<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.0/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>

<script>
    // Navbar scroll effect
    const navbar = document.querySelector('.navbar');
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (window.scrollY > 50) {
            navbar.classList.add('scrolled');
        } else {
            navbar.classList.remove('scrolled');
        }
    });

    // Scroll to top functionality
    const scrollTopBtn = document.getElementById('scrollTop');
    
    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (window.scrollY > 300) {
            scrollTopBtn.classList.add('visible');
        } else {
            scrollTopBtn.classList.remove('visible');
        }
    });

    scrollTopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        window.scrollTo({
            top: 0,
            behavior: 'smooth'
        });
    });

    // Auto-dismiss alerts after 5 seconds
    const alerts = document.querySelectorAll('.alert');
    alerts.forEach(alert => {
        setTimeout(() => {
            const bsAlert = new bootstrap.Alert(alert);
            bsAlert.close();
        }, 5000);
    });

    // Sidebar Toggle Logic
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
    const toggleBtn = document.querySelector('.navbar-toggler');
    
    if (toggleBtn && sidebar) {
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            sidebar.classList.toggle('open');
        });
        
        // Close sidebar when clicking outside on mobile
        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (window.innerWidth <= 991 && sidebar.classList.contains('open')) {
                if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !toggleBtn.contains(e.target)) {
                    sidebar.classList.remove('open');
                }
            }
        });
    }

</script>

{% block extra_js %}{% endblock %}

<script>
// Dark Mode Toggle
(function() {
    const btn = document.getElementById('darkModeToggle');
    const icon = document.getElementById('darkModeIcon');
    const html = document.documentElement;

    function applyTheme(dark) {
        if (dark) {
            html.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
            icon.className = 'bi bi-sun-fill';
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        } else {
            html.removeAttribute('data-theme');
            icon.className = 'bi bi-moon-stars-fill';
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
        }
    }

    // Set initial icon based on saved preference
    if (localStorage.getItem('theme') === 'dark') {
        icon.className = 'bi bi-sun-fill';
    }

    if (btn) {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const isDark = html.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
            applyTheme(!isDark);
        });
    }
})();
</script>


{% if user.is_authenticated %}
<script>
// Notification system
let notificationInterval;

function fetchNotifications() {
    fetch('/notifications/api/')
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            updateNotificationBadge(data.unread_count);
            updateNotificationList(data.notifications);
        })
        .catch(error => console.error('Error fetching notifications:', error));
}

function updateNotificationBadge(count) {
    const badge = document.getElementById('notification-badge');
    if (count > 0) {
        badge.textContent = count;
        badge.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        badge.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

function updateNotificationList(notifications) {
    const listDiv = document.getElementById('notification-list');
    
    if (notifications.length === 0) {
        listDiv.innerHTML = '<li class="dropdown-item text-center" style="color: var(--dust-light) !important;"><small>No notifications</small></li>';
        return;
    }
    
    listDiv.innerHTML = notifications.map(n => `
        <li>
            <a class="dropdown-item ${n.read ? '' : 'bg-light'}" 
               href="${n.link || '#'}" 
               onclick="markAsRead(${n.id}); return false;">
                <strong>${n.title}</strong>
                <p class="mb-0 small">${n.message}</p>
                <small>${new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleString()}</small>
            </a>
        </li>
        <li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li>
    `).join('');
}

function markAsRead(notificationId) {
    fetch(`/notifications/api/${notificationId}/read/`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
            'X-CSRFToken': '{{ csrf_token }}'
        }
    })
    .then(() => fetchNotifications())
    .catch(error => console.error('Error marking as read:', error));
}

// Fetch notifications immediately and every 30 seconds
fetchNotifications();
notificationInterval = setInterval(fetchNotifications, 30000);
</script>
{% endif %}

<!-- ===== Global Password Show/Hide Toggle ===== -->
<script>
(function () {
    function wrapPasswordInputs() {
        document.querySelectorAll('input[type="password"]').forEach(function (input) {
            // Don't double-wrap
            if (input.dataset.eyeWrapped) return;
            input.dataset.eyeWrapped = '1';

            // Build wrapper
            var wrapper = document.createElement('div');
            wrapper.style.cssText = 'position:relative;display:flex;align-items:center;';

            // Clone input styles — keep it inside a flex row
            input.style.paddingRight = '2.6rem';
            input.style.flex = '1';

            // Eye button
            var btn = document.createElement('button');
            btn.type = 'button';
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Toggle password visibility');
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-eye"></i>';
            btn.style.cssText = [
                'position:absolute',
                'right:0.6rem',
                'top:50%',
                'transform:translateY(-50%)',
                'background:none',
                'border:none',
                'padding:0.25rem 0.4rem',
                'cursor:pointer',
                'color:#aaa',
                'font-size:1rem',
                'line-height:1',
                'z-index:5',
            ].join(';');

            btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                var isHidden = input.type === 'password';
                input.type = isHidden ? 'text' : 'password';
                btn.querySelector('i').className = isHidden ? 'bi bi-eye-slash' : 'bi bi-eye';
                btn.style.color = isHidden ? '#00D084' : '#aaa';
            });

            // Insert wrapper
            input.parentNode.insertBefore(wrapper, input);
            wrapper.appendChild(input);
            wrapper.appendChild(btn);
        });
    }

    // Run once DOM is ready
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', wrapPasswordInputs);
    } else {
        wrapPasswordInputs();
    }
})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Show/hide farmer fields based on user type
    const userTypeRadios = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="user_type"]');
    const farmerFields = document.getElementById('farmerFields');
    
    userTypeRadios.forEach(radio => {
        radio.addEventListener('change', function() {
            if (this.value === 'farmer') {
                farmerFields.style.display = 'block';
                // Make farmer fields required
                document.querySelector('input[name="farm_name"]').required = true;
                document.querySelector('input[name="specialization"]').required = true;
            } else {
                farmerFields.style.display = 'none';
                // Make farmer fields not required
                document.querySelector('input[name="farm_name"]').required = false;
                document.querySelector('input[name="specialization"]').required = false;
            }
        });
    });
    
    // Initialize on page load
    if (document.getElementById('farmer').checked) {
        farmerFields.style.display = 'block';
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                                        <span class="badge bg-warning text-dark mt-1">Save {{ pool.input_item.group_discount_percentage }}%!</span>
                                    {% endif %}

                            </div>

                        <p>{{ pool.input_item.usage_instructions }}</p>
                        {% endif %}

                            <strong><i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle"></i> Safety Warnings:</strong>
                            <p class="mb-0 mt-1">{{ pool.input_item.safety_warnings }}</p>
                        </div>

                                    {{ p.farmer.username }}
                                    {% if p.farmer == pool.organizer %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-warning text-dark ms-1">Organizer</span>
                                    {% endif %}

                        <p class="mb-0"><strong>Supplier:</strong><br>{{ pool.input_item.supplier.username }}</p>

                            <i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill"></i> <strong>You're in this pool!</strong><br>
                            Your quantity: <strong>{{ user_participation.quantity }} {{ pool.input_item.unit }}</strong>
                        </div>
                        {% if pool.status == 'open' and user != pool.organizer %}

                            <i class="bi bi-box-arrow-left"></i> Leave Pool
                        </a>
                        {% endif %}
                        {% if user == pool.organizer and pool.status == 'open' %}

                            <i class="bi bi-x-circle"></i> Cancel Pool
                        </a>
                        {% endif %}

                            <div class="card-body text-center">
                                <h5>Join This Pool</h5>
                                <p class="text-muted small">
                                    Save <strong>{{ pool.input_item.group_discount_percentage }}%</strong> by joining!
                                </p>
                                <a href="{% url 'inputs:join_group_buy' pool.id %}" class="btn btn-warning w-100 btn-lg">
                                    <i class="bi bi-plus-circle-fill"></i> Join Group Buy
                                </a>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    {% else %}
                        <div class="alert alert-secondary">
                            <i class="bi bi-lock"></i> This pool is no longer accepting participants.
                        </div>
                    {% endif %}
                {% else %}
                    <div class="alert alert-warning">

                    </div>
                {% endif %}

                <!-- Share Card -->

{% block title %}Explore Districts - Smart Agricultural Marketplace{% endblock %}

{% block extra_css %}

{% block content %}
<div class="container py-4">
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <div>
            <h2 class="fw-bold mb-1">Explore Districts</h2>
            <p class="text-secondary mb-0">Find farmers and products by region</p>
        </div>
        <a href="{% url 'marketplace:product_list' %}" class="btn btn-outline-success">
            <i class="bi bi-basket me-2"></i> Browse All Products
        </a>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-4">
        <!-- Map Column -->
        <div class="col-lg-8">
            <div class="map-container">
                <div id="map" style="height: 100%; width: 100%;"></div>
                <button id="location-btn" onclick="findMyLocation()">
                    <i class="bi bi-crosshair me-1"></i> Use My Location
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Districts List Column -->
        <div class="col-lg-4">
            <div class="district-list-card card border-0 shadow-sm p-3 h-100">
                <h5 class="fw-bold mb-3">Districts by Region</h5>
                
                {% for region, districts in region_data.items %}
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <div class="region-header" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#collapse-{{ region|slugify }}">
                        <span>{{ region }} Region</span>
                        <span class="badge bg-white text-success rounded-pill">{{ districts|length }}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="collapse show" id="collapse-{{ region|slugify }}">
                        <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                            {% for district in districts %}
                                <div class="district-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center" 
                                     onclick="flyToDistrict('{{ district.name }}')">
                                    <span>{{ district.name }}</span>
                                    <span class="badge bg-light text-dark border">{{ district.count }} Farmers</span>
                                </div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<!-- Leaflet JS -->
<script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js" integrity="sha256-20nQCchB9co0qIjJZRGuk2/Z9VM+kNiyxNV1lvTlZBo=" crossorigin=""></script>
<script>
    // Initialize Map centered on Uganda
    var map = L.map('map').setView([1.3733, 32.2903], 7);

    // Add OpenStreetMap tiles
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
        maxZoom: 19,
        attribution: '© OpenStreetMap'
    }).addTo(map);

    // District Coordinates Data
    const districtCoords = {{ coordinates|safe }};
    const markers = {};

    // Add Markers
    for (const [name, coords] of Object.entries(districtCoords)) {
        if (coords.lat && coords.lng) {
            const marker = L.marker([coords.lat, coords.lng]).addTo(map);
            marker.bindPopup(`
                <div class="text-center">
                    <h6 class="fw-bold mb-1">${name}</h6>
                    <a href="{% url 'marketplace:product_list' %}?location=${name}" class="btn btn-sm btn-success mt-2">View Products</a>
                </div>
            `);
            markers[name] = marker;
        }
    }

    // Function to fly to district
    function flyToDistrict(districtName) {
        const coords = districtCoords[districtName];
        if (coords) {
            map.flyTo([coords.lat, coords.lng], 12);
            if (markers[districtName]) {
                markers[districtName].openPopup();
            }
        } else {
            // If coordinate missing, try to search or just alert
            // For now, search OpenStreetMap generic search? No, simpler to just log.
            console.log("No coordinates for " + districtName);
            // Fallback: search products directly
            window.location.href = "{% url 'marketplace:product_list' %}?location=" + districtName;
        }
    }

    // Geolocation
    function findMyLocation() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('location-btn');
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-hourglass-split"></i> Locating...';
        
        map.locate({setView: true, maxZoom: 10});
        
        map.on('locationfound', function(e) {
            var radius = e.accuracy / 2;
            L.marker(e.latlng).addTo(map)
                .bindPopup("You are within " + radius + " meters from this point").openPopup();
            L.circle(e.latlng, radius).addTo(map);
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-check-lg"></i> Found You!';
            
            // Optional: Reverse geocode to find district name?
            // Leaflet doesn't do this natively without plugins/API.
        });

        map.on('locationerror', function(e) {
            alert(e.message);
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-crosshair"></i> Retry';
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Show/hide urgent fields
    const isUrgentCheckbox = document.getElementById('isUrgent');
    const urgentFields = document.getElementById('urgentFields');
    
    isUrgentCheckbox.addEventListener('change', function() {
        if (this.checked) {
            urgentFields.style.display = 'block';
        } else {
            urgentFields.style.display = 'none';
        }
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    <script>
    (function(){
        var toast = document.getElementById('welcome-toast');
        var bar   = document.getElementById('toastProgressBar');
        var closeBtn = document.getElementById('toastClose');
        var buyBtn   = document.getElementById('toastBuyBtn');
        var duration = 10000; // ms

        function dismissToast() {
            toast.classList.add('toast-hidden');
        }

        // Kick off the progress bar shrink
        // Use a tiny delay so the transition fires after paint
        setTimeout(function(){
            bar.style.transition = 'width ' + (duration / 1000) + 's linear';
            bar.style.width = '0%';
        }, 50);

        // Auto-dismiss
        var autoTimer = setTimeout(dismissToast, duration);

        closeBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
            clearTimeout(autoTimer);
            dismissToast();
        });

        // Also dismiss when navigating via the button (optional UX)
        buyBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
            clearTimeout(autoTimer);
            dismissToast();
        });
    })();
    </script>

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        // Auto-dismiss welcome banner
        const banner = document.querySelector('.welcome-banner');
        if (banner) {
            setTimeout(() => {
                // Set explicit height for transition to work
                banner.style.height = banner.scrollHeight + 'px';
                banner.style.transition = 'all 0.8s ease-in-out';
                banner.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                
                // Force reflow
                void banner.offsetHeight;

                // Collapse
                banner.style.opacity = '0';
                banner.style.height = '0';
                banner.style.marginTop = '0';
                banner.style.marginBottom = '0';
                banner.style.padding = '0';
                banner.style.border = 'none';
                
                setTimeout(() => {
                    banner.remove();
                }, 800);
            }, 4000); // Reduced to 4 seconds for faster feedback
        }
    });

// Weather widget helper for tabs
function showWeatherSpecific(districtName) {
    const d = getSimulatedWeather(districtName);
    updateWeatherUI(d);
    
    // Manual click pauses auto-rotation for 30s? (Optional, let's just update)
    // Find matching index in list to continue from there
    const newIdx = allDistrictsData.findIndex(item => item.name === districtName);
    if (newIdx !== -1) weatherIndex = newIdx;
}

function updateWeatherUI(d) {
    const weatherBlock = document.querySelector('.weather-district');
    const statsBlock   = document.querySelector('.weather-stats');

    // Fade out
    [weatherBlock, statsBlock].forEach(el => { if (el) el.style.opacity = '0'; });

    setTimeout(() => {
        // Update elements
        const distEl = document.getElementById('weather-district');
        const tempEl = document.getElementById('weather-temp');
        const condEl = document.getElementById('weather-condition');
        const humEl  = document.getElementById('weather-humidity');
        const windEl = document.getElementById('weather-wind');
        const farmEl = document.getElementById('weather-farming');
        const regEl  = document.getElementById('weather-region');

        if (distEl) distEl.textContent = d.district;
        if (regEl)  regEl.textContent  = d.region + ' Region';
        if (tempEl) tempEl.textContent = d.temp;
        if (condEl) condEl.innerHTML   = `<i class="bi ${d.icon}"></i> ${d.condition}`;
        if (humEl)  humEl.textContent  = d.humidity;
        if (windEl) windEl.textContent = d.wind;
        if (farmEl) {
            farmEl.textContent = d.farming;
            farmEl.style.color = d.farming === 'Excellent' ? '#43A047'
                               : d.farming === 'Good'      ? '#7CB342' : '#FB8C00';
        }

        // Fade in
        [weatherBlock, statsBlock].forEach(el => { if (el) el.style.opacity = '1'; });
    }, 250); // half the CSS transition time
}

// Mobile sidebar toggle
const sidebarToggle = document.getElementById('sidebarToggle');
const sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');

sidebarToggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
    sidebar.classList.toggle('open');
});

// Close sidebar when clicking outside on mobile
document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (window.innerWidth <= 991) {
        if (!sidebar.contains(e.target) && !sidebarToggle.contains(e.target)) {
            sidebar.classList.remove('open');
        }
    }
});

// Auto-rotate weather districts
let weatherIndex = 0;
let weatherInterval;

// Flatten districts from context
const allDistrictsData = [];
{% for region, districts in all_districts.items %}
    {% for district in districts %}
        allDistrictsData.push({
            name: "{{ district }}",
            region: "{{ region }}"
        });
    {% endfor %}
{% endfor %}

function getSimulatedWeather(districtName) {
    // Generate somewhat realistic weather based on district/random
    const temps = [24, 25, 26, 27, 28, 29, 30, 31, 32];
    const conditions = ['Sunny', 'Partly Cloudy', 'Cloudy', 'Light Rain', 'Clear'];
    const icons = ['bi-sun', 'bi-cloud-sun', 'bi-cloud', 'bi-cloud-drizzle', 'bi-brightness-high'];
    const farming = ['Excellent', 'Good', 'Fair'];
    
    // Consistent details for specific districts
    let tempIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * temps.length);
    let condIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * conditions.length);
    
    if (districtName === 'Kampala') { tempIdx = 4; condIdx = 1; } // 28C, Partly Cloudy
    
    return {
        district: districtName,
        temp: temps[tempIdx] + '°C',
        condition: conditions[condIdx],
        icon: icons[condIdx],
        humidity: (60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30)) + '%',
        wind: (5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 15)) + 'km/h',
        farming: farming[Math.floor(Math.random() * farming.length)]
    };
}

// Override showWeatherSpecific to stop rotation
const originalShowWeather = window.showWeatherSpecific || function() {};
window.showWeatherSpecific = function(name) {
    if (weatherInterval) clearInterval(weatherInterval);
    
    // If original function exists (it should), call it
    // But we need to make sure we use the correct logic
    // We can just call updateWeatherUI directly with new data
    const d = getSimulatedWeather(name);
    // Find region if possible
    const found = allDistrictsData.find(x => x.name === name);
    if (found) d.region = found.region;
    
    updateWeatherUI(d);
};

// Initial state — Start from User District if available
if (allDistrictsData.length > 0) {
    const userDistrict = "{{ user.district|default:'' }}";
    
    if (userDistrict) {
        let idx = allDistrictsData.findIndex(d => d.name === userDistrict);
        
        // Handle spelling mismatches
        if (idx === -1) {
            const mapping = { 'Luwero': 'Luweero', 'Bukwo': 'Bukwa' };
            if (mapping[userDistrict]) {
                idx = allDistrictsData.findIndex(d => d.name === mapping[userDistrict]);
            }
        }

        if (idx !== -1) {
            weatherIndex = idx;
        } else {
            // Not found? Add dynamic entry so it displays correctly
            allDistrictsData.unshift({ name: userDistrict, region: 'Local' });
            weatherIndex = 0;
        }
    } else {
        // Default to Kampala if available
        const kIdx = allDistrictsData.findIndex(d => d.name === 'Kampala');
        if (kIdx !== -1) weatherIndex = kIdx;
    }

    let currentWeather = getSimulatedWeather(allDistrictsData[weatherIndex].name);
    currentWeather.region = allDistrictsData[weatherIndex].region;
    updateWeatherUI(currentWeather);

    // Rotate every 5 seconds
    weatherInterval = setInterval(() => {
        weatherIndex = (weatherIndex + 1) % allDistrictsData.length;
        const d = getSimulatedWeather(allDistrictsData[weatherIndex].name);
        d.region = allDistrictsData[weatherIndex].region;
        updateWeatherUI(d);
    }, 5000);
}

</script>



{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.0/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.price-chart').forEach(canvas => {
    const min = parseFloat(canvas.dataset.min) || 0;
    const avg = parseFloat(canvas.dataset.avg) || 0;
    const max = parseFloat(canvas.dataset.max) || 0;

    new Chart(canvas, {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
            labels: ['Min', 'Avg', 'Max'],
            datasets: [{
                data: [min, avg, max],
                backgroundColor: [
                    'rgba(220,53,69,0.7)',
                    'rgba(13,110,253,0.7)',
                    'rgba(25,135,84,0.7)'
                ],
                borderRadius: 4,
                borderSkipped: false,
            }]
        },
        options: {
            responsive: false,
            plugins: { legend: { display: false }, tooltip: {
                callbacks: {
                    label: ctx => 'UGX ' + ctx.raw.toLocaleString()
                }
            }},
            scales: {
                x: { display: false },
                y: { display: false }
            }
        }
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    function changeImage(imageUrl) {
        const img = document.getElementById('mainImage');
        img.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => {
            img.src = imageUrl;
            img.style.opacity = '1';
        }, 200);
        // Highlight active thumbnail
        document.querySelectorAll('.thumb-img').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('border-success', 'border-3'));
        event.target.classList.add('border-success', 'border-3');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<style>
/* ── Order Status Timeline ── */
.order-timeline { padding: 8px 0 16px; }

.timeline-track {
    position: absolute;
    top: 20px;
    left: 10%;
    right: 10%;
    height: 3px;
    background: #dee2e6;
    z-index: 0;
}

.timeline-step {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 1;
    text-align: center;
    flex: 1;
}

.step-circle {
    width: 42px;
    height: 42px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: #dee2e6;
    color: #adb5bd;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    margin: 0 auto 8px;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    border: 3px solid #fff;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #dee2e6;
    transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.timeline-step.active .step-circle {
    background: #198754;
    color: #fff;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #198754;
}

.timeline-step.current .step-circle {
    background: #198754;
    color: #fff;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(25,135,84,0.25);
    animation: stepPulse 1.8s ease-in-out infinite;
}

@keyframes stepPulse {
    0%, 100% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(25,135,84,0.25); }
    50%       { box-shadow: 0 0 0 8px rgba(25,135,84,0.1); }
}

.step-label {
    font-size: 0.75rem;
    font-weight: 600;
    color: #adb5bd;
}
.timeline-step.active .step-label { color: #198754; }

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Calculate total amount when quantity changes
    const quantityInput = document.getElementById('quantity');
    const totalAmountInput = document.getElementById('totalAmount');
    const pricePerUnit = {{ product.price }};
    
    quantityInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
        const quantity = parseInt(this.value) || 1;
        const total = quantity * pricePerUnit;
        totalAmountInput.value = total.toLocaleString('en-UG', {minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 0});
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

    {name:'Rubirizi',region:'Western'},{name:'Rukiga',region:'Western'},{name:'Rukungiri',region:'Western'},
    {name:'Sheema',region:'Western'},{name:'Rwampara',region:'Western'},
];

const CONDITIONS = [
    {label:'Sunny',emoji:'☀️'},{label:'Partly Cloudy',emoji:'⛅'},
    {label:'Cloudy',emoji:'☁️'},{label:'Light Rain',emoji:'🌧️'},
    {label:'Clear Sky',emoji:'🌤️'},{label:'Scattered Showers',emoji:'🌦️'},
    {label:'Heavy Rain',emoji:'⛈️'},{label:'Misty',emoji:'🌫️'},
];

function simulatedWeather(district) {
    const seed     = district.name.charCodeAt(0) + district.name.charCodeAt(district.name.length - 1);
    const condIdx  = seed % CONDITIONS.length;
    return {
        name:      district.name,
        region:    district.region,
        emoji:     CONDITIONS[condIdx].emoji,
        condition: CONDITIONS[condIdx].label,
        temp:      (22 + (seed % 12)) + '°C',
        humidity:  (55 + (seed % 35)) + '%',
        wind:      (4  + (seed % 18)) + 'km/h',
    };
}

const NUM_CARDS   = 6;
const INTERVAL_MS = 5000;
const STAGGER_MS  = Math.round(INTERVAL_MS / NUM_CARDS); // ~833ms
const cardIndices = [];

for (let i = 0; i < NUM_CARDS; i++) {
    cardIndices.push(Math.round(i * ALL_DISTRICTS.length / NUM_CARDS));
}

function renderCard(cardEl, districtObj) {
    const inner   = cardEl.querySelector('.card-inner');
    const weather = simulatedWeather(districtObj);
    inner.classList.add('fading');
    setTimeout(() => {
        inner.querySelector('.weather-emoji').textContent   = weather.emoji;
        inner.querySelector('.district-name').textContent   = weather.name;
        inner.querySelector('.district-region').textContent = weather.region + ' Region';
        inner.querySelector('.district-temp').textContent   = weather.temp;
        inner.querySelector('.district-condition').textContent = weather.condition;
        const spans = inner.querySelectorAll('.district-stats span');
        if (spans[0]) spans[0].innerHTML = `<i class="bi bi-droplet"></i> ${weather.humidity}`;
        if (spans[1]) spans[1].innerHTML = `<i class="bi bi-wind"></i> ${weather.wind}`;
        inner.classList.remove('fading');
    }, 300);
}

const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.district-weather-card');

// Initial render — each card starts at a different district
cards.forEach((card, i) => renderCard(card, ALL_DISTRICTS[cardIndices[i]]));

// Staggered rotation: card i starts its interval with i * STAGGER_MS delay
cards.forEach((card, i) => {
    setTimeout(() => {
        setInterval(() => {
            cardIndices[i] = (cardIndices[i] + 1) % ALL_DISTRICTS.length;
            renderCard(card, ALL_DISTRICTS[cardIndices[i]]);
        }, INTERVAL_MS);
    }, i * STAGGER_MS);
});
</script>
{% endblock %}

    list_filter = ['alert_type', 'severity', 'is_active']

FILE: .\weather\models.py

from django.db import models

class WeatherAlert(models.Model):
    """
    Weather alerts and warnings for farmers
    """
    ALERT_TYPES = (
        ('rain', 'Heavy Rain'),
        ('drought', 'Drought Warning'),
        ('storm', 'Storm Warning'),
        ('frost', 'Frost Warning'),
        ('heatwave', 'Heatwave'),
    )
    
    SEVERITY_LEVELS = (
        ('low', 'Low'),
        ('medium', 'Medium'),
        ('high', 'High'),
        ('critical', 'Critical'),
    )
    
    alert_type = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=ALERT_TYPES)
    severity = models.CharField(max_length=20, choices=SEVERITY_LEVELS)
    
    title = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    description = models.TextField()
    affected_regions = models.CharField(max_length=300, help_text="Comma-separated regions")
    
    start_date = models.DateTimeField()
    end_date = models.DateTimeField()
    
    recommendations = models.TextField(help_text="What farmers should do")
    
    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.get_alert_type_display()} - {self.title}"
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Weather Alert"
        verbose_name_plural = "Weather Alerts"
        ordering = ['-created_at']


class PlantingSeason(models.Model):
    """
    Planting season recommendations based on climate data
    """
    crop_name = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    region = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    
    best_planting_start = models.DateField()
    best_planting_end = models.DateField()
    
    expected_harvest_start = models.DateField()
    expected_harvest_end = models.DateField()
    
    rainfall_required = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="e.g., 800-1200mm")
    temperature_range = models.CharField(max_length=100, help_text="e.g., 20-30°C")
    
    planting_tips = models.TextField()
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.crop_name} - {self.region}"
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Planting Season"
        verbose_name_plural = "Planting Seasons"


class PestAlert(models.Model):
    """
    Pest and disease outbreak alerts
    """
    pest_name = models.CharField(max_length=200)
    affected_crops = models.CharField(max_length=300, help_text="Comma-separated crops")
    affected_regions = models.CharField(max_length=300)
    
    description = models.TextField()
    symptoms = models.TextField(help_text="How to identify the pest/disease")
    
    severity = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=[('low', 'Low'), ('medium', 'Medium'), ('high', 'High')],
        default='medium'
    )
    
    control_measures = models.TextField(help_text="How to control/prevent")
    recommended_products = models.TextField(blank=True, help_text="Recommended pesticides")
    
    image = models.ImageField(upload_to='pests/', blank=True, null=True)
    
    reported_date = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    
    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.pest_name} - {self.affected_regions}"
    
    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Pest Alert"
        verbose_name_plural = "Pest Alerts"
        ordering = ['-reported_date']

from django.urls import path
from . import views

app_name = 'weather'

urlpatterns = [
    # Main Climate Suite Dashboard
    path('', views.climate_suite, name='climate_suite'),
    
    # API endpoint for the JavaScript auto-rotation data
    path('api/get-weather/', views.get_weather_api, name='get_weather_api'),
    
    # Pest Alert details
    path('pest/<int:pk>/', views.pest_alert_detail, name='pest_alert_detail'),
]

from django.shortcuts import render, get_object_or_404
from django.utils import timezone
from django.http import JsonResponse
from .models import WeatherAlert, PlantingSeason, PestAlert
from .services.farming_advisor import FarmingAdvisor
import requests
from datetime import datetime, date

def climate_suite(request):
    """
    Enhanced climate suite dashboard with weather, planting seasons, pest alerts,
    and intelligent farming recommendations
    """
    active_alerts = WeatherAlert.objects.filter(
        is_active=True,
        end_date__gte=timezone.now()
    ).order_by('-severity')
    
    planting_seasons = PlantingSeason.objects.all()[:6]
    pest_alerts = PestAlert.objects.filter(is_active=True)[:6]
    
    # List of districts for the auto-rotating JavaScript dashboard
    featured_districts = ['Kampala', 'Entebbe', 'Mbarara', 'Gulu', 'Jinja', 'Mbale']
    
    # Get initial weather for user's location or default to Kampala
    user_location = request.user.location if request.user.is_authenticated and hasattr(request.user, 'location') and request.user.location else 'Kampala'
    weather_data = get_weather_data(user_location)
    
    # Initialize Farming Advisor
    advisor = FarmingAdvisor()
    
    # Generate intelligent recommendations
    planting_recommendations = []
    spray_recommendation = {}
    daily_activities = []
    
    if weather_data:
        planting_recommendations = advisor.get_planting_recommendations(user_location, weather_data)
        spray_recommendation = advisor.get_spray_recommendations(user_location, weather_data)
        daily_activities = advisor.get_daily_activities(weather_data)
    
    # Sample harvest predictions (in real app, get from user's planted crops)
    sample_planted_crops = [
        {'crop': 'maize', 'planted_date': date(2026, 1, 15)},
        {'crop': 'beans', 'planted_date': date(2026, 2, 1)},
    ]
    harvest_predictions = advisor.get_harvest_predictions(sample_planted_crops)
    
    context = {
        'active_alerts': active_alerts,
        'planting_seasons': planting_seasons,
        'pest_alerts': pest_alerts,
        'weather_data': weather_data,
        'featured_districts': featured_districts,
        # New intelligent recommendations
        'planting_recommendations': planting_recommendations,
        'spray_recommendation': spray_recommendation,
        'daily_activities': daily_activities,
        'harvest_predictions': harvest_predictions,
    }
    
    return render(request, 'weather/climate_suite.html', context)

def get_weather_api(request):
    """
    API endpoint for JavaScript to fetch weather for a specific district
    Usage: /weather/api/get-weather/?district=Gulu
    """
    district = request.GET.get('district', 'Kampala')
    data = get_weather_data(district)
    
    if data:
        return JsonResponse(data)
    return JsonResponse({'error': 'Could not fetch weather data'}, status=400)

def get_weather_data(location):
    """
    Fetch weather data from OpenWeatherMap API
    """
    API_KEY = 'be5fb80ee9e2e0af2f0292ec2d628012'
    BASE_URL = 'http://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather'
    
    try:
        params = {
            'q': f"{location},UG",
            'appid': API_KEY,
            'units': 'metric'
        }
        
        response = requests.get(BASE_URL, params=params, timeout=5)
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            data = response.json()
            return {
                'temperature': data['main']['temp'],
                'feels_like': data['main']['feels_like'],
                'humidity': data['main']['humidity'],
                'description': data['weather'][0]['description'],
                'icon': data['weather'][0]['icon'],
                'wind_speed': data['wind']['speed'],
            }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Weather API Error: {e}")
    
    return None

def pest_alert_detail(request, pk):
    pest_alert = get_object_or_404(PestAlert, pk=pk)
    return render(request, 'weather/pest_alert_detail.html', {'pest_alert': pest_alert})
